the row itself explains ephemeral vs provisioned |\n| `AXON_REQUIRE_CERTS` | `cert_gate.rs:37`; `enforce_or_exit` called from `cmd_goal/session/run/test/deploy` **and `build_ir_and_link` (main.rs:6892)** | CONSISTENT — the certified obligation is the compiler's mint lowering, so the build-time check is the right place and it is wired into the native path |\n| `AXON_TEE_ENCLAVE`, `AXON_TEE_MEASUREMENT` | **not in `ALL_ENV_VARS` at all** — see below | see below |\n\n---\n\n## Priority 6 — resource budget\n\n`AXON_BUDGET_TOKENS` is rowed under Priority 1. `AXON_OS_TIMEOUT_MS` (`axon-os/runtime.rs:84`), `AXON_VM_TIMEOUT_SECS` / `AXON_VM_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_SECS` (`axon-vm/src/main.rs:2413/2293`), `AXON_INTENT_TIMEOUT_MS` (`axon-intent/src/synth.rs:176`), `AXON_CORTEX_GENERATOR_TIMEOUT_MS` (`axon-cortex/src/generate.rs:324`) are all **N-A**: each bounds a subprocess *its own supervisor* spawned, so no second engine could read them. `AXON_PROOF_TIMEOUT_MS` / `AXON_PROOF_DEPTH` (`smt.rs:634/596`) are **CONSISTENT** — the SMT pipeline is shared front-end, ahead of the engine split.\n\n## Priority 7 — guest boot policy\n\n`axon-guest-init` reads exactly one var (`AXON_GUEST_ALLOW_NO_POLICY`, rowed above) and **sets** five: `AXON_PRINCIPAL`, `AXON_BUDGET_TOKENS`, `AXON_RUN_ID`, `AXON_ALLOWED_EFFECTS`, `AXON_SOURCE_HASH` (`main.rs:167-181`). Two of the five — `AXON_RUN_ID` and `AXON_SOURCE_HASH` — are read by **no shipped code anywhere** and are not registry rows. The comment above them says \"Export identity to the Axon runtime (provenance log, `ai_complete` tier)\"; no runtime reads them. Dead exports, not a security hole, but the comment claims a consumer that does not exist.\n\n## Grouped: developer-convenience and diagnostics (no trust consequence)\n\nNine rows. Each is either a *driver-side* path/credential with no second engine, or a debug toggle whose output nothing gates on:\n\n`AXON_AI_PROVIDER`, `AXON_AI_BASE_URL`, `AXON_AI_API_KEY`, `AXON_DOTENV`, `AXON_DOTENV_WALK` (all `axon-ai/src/lib.rs:232/242/268/67/82) — linked into **both** engines, so CONSISTENT, and they configure *where* a call goes, not *whether* it is allowed. `AXON_VM_DEBUG` / `AXON_VM_QUIET` (`axon-vm/main.rs:2595/2272`), `AXON_CI_NO_KVM` (`axon-vm`, `axon-attest`, `axon-web`) — verbosity and a CI mock switch. `AXON_CONFIG_DIR` (`axon-vm/main.rs:3281`) — redirects where the VM reads its Principal registry; I classify it as convenience *only because* it is VM-launcher-side, but note it is the closest of this group to a trust knob. `AXON_BIN` (`axon-os`, `axon-intent`, `axon-web`), `AXON_INTENT_GEN`, `AXON_DUMP_BINDINGS`/`AXON_DUMP_SHAPES` (`interp.rs:2423/2426` — session substrate, interp-only by construction), `AXON_AI_MOCK` (`axon-ai/src/lib.rs:365` **and** `interp.rs:3572` — CONSISTENT across engines), `AXON_AI_MODEL_BALANCED` (`axon-ai/src/lib.rs:691`, both engines), `AXON_AI_MODEL_CHEAP`/`_STRONG` (`ai_routing.rs:261-263`, interp-only but codegen **E0910-refuses** any non-`balanced` tier at `codegen/mod.rs:1098` — REFUSED, not divergent), `AXON_GOAL_CONTINUE` (`interp/provenance.rs:283`; `axon-rt/src/goal.rs` has no `AXON_*` read, but it is set only by `axon goal --iterate`, an interpreter-only verb), `AXON_TEST_DOTENV_VAR`/`_NEW` (fixtures).\n\n---\n\n## Ranked SILENT-DIVERGENCE list\n\n**1. `AXON_ALLOWED_EFFECTS` / `AXON_BUDGET_TOKENS` at *run* time on a prebuilt native binary** (D-002, but wider than the tracked guest case).\nExperiment: build `fn main() -> i64 { println(\"IO HAPPENED\") 0 }` with **no** ceiling set (`axon build c.ax -o cbin`). Then run `AXON_ALLOWED_EFFECTS=Pure axon run c.ax` (expect exit 8) and `AXON_ALLOWED_EFFECTS=Pure ./cbin` (expect \"IO HAPPENED\", exit 0). Same program, same variable, two engines; the build-time refusal is bypassed because the build happened before the ceiling existed. Repeat with `AXON_BUDGET_TOKENS=0` and a program calling `ai_complete` under `AXON_AI_MOCK=1`. The fix is one line: add both names to the `axon-rt/src/lib.rs:2030` loop, which already exists and already fires at run time for three sibling vars.\n\n**2. `AXON_AI_REPLAY` on native.**\nExperiment: a program whose only effect is `ai_complete(\"say hi\")`. Run `AXON_AI_REPLAY=c.cache axon run p.ax` twice — second run makes no network call. Build it and run `AXON_AI_REPLAY=c.cache ./pbin` twice with a real `AXON_AI_API_KEY` and no `AXON_AI_MOCK`: observe two live HTTP requests and (with a non-zero temperature) two different responses, exit 0 both times, cache file unchanged. A cheaper variant that needs no key: point `AXON_AI_BASE_URL` at a local listener and count connections. The claim under test is the registry's own words, \"no live call\".\n\n**3. `AXON_SEED` and the three refusals on `wasm32-wasip1`.**\nExperiment: `axon target build --engine codegen --target wasm32 r.ax` for a program printing `random_i64()`, then run the artifact under `AXON_WASM_RT` with `AXON_SEED=1` and `AXON_SEED=999` — observe the same value twice (and the same value as a third unseeded run), where interp varies. Separately run the wasm artifact under `AXON_REPLAY=j` and observe it does *not* print the exit-2 refusal that the native binary does. Both follow from the `target_is_wasm` early returns at `codegen/asi.rs:1001` and `:1041`.\n\n**4. `AXON_PRINCIPAL` / audit attribution on native.**\nExperiment: same `ai_complete` program under `AXON_AI_MOCK=1`. `AXON_PRINCIPAL=alice axon run p.ax` then `axon trace --ai --json` — one `ai_call` record with `\"principal\":\"alice\"`. Build and `AXON_PRINCIPAL=alice ./pbin`, then `axon trace --ai --json` — expect zero new records. The native binary spends tokens and leaves no audit row, under any principal.\n\n---\n\n## Two findings outside the requested table, because they undercut its premise\n\n**The both-directions registry gate has a blind spot, and a trust-category var is in it.** `env_registry.rs` `vars_read()` scans only for the literals `env::var(\"` and `env::var_os(\"` (plus a `const NAME: &str = \"AXON_…\"` shape). `tee_in_enclave` and `tee_attest_measurement` read `AXON_TEE_ENCLAVE` and `AXON_TEE_MEASUREMENT` through the host seam — `crate::host::with_host(|h| h.env_var(\"AXON_TEE_ENCLAVE\"))` at `interp/builtins.rs:3531` and `:3538`. Neither matches the scan, neither has a registry row, and neither appears in `AXON_REFERENCE.md`. `AXON_TEE_ENCLAVE=1` is what makes `tee_in_enclave()` return true — an attestation-category signal that the enumeration tool reports as not existing. (Reading via the host seam is otherwise the *better* choice: it means these two are recorded and replayed, unlike every `std::env::var` read.)\n\n**`scripts/replay_host_gate.sh` contains no reference to `build` or `native`.** It is the named gate for `AXON_RECORD`/`AXON_REPLAY`, and the native refusal added for those vars is pinned by exactly one test, `cli_run.rs:31548`. There is no harness-level coverage of engine parity for *any* environment variable; the three new tests in `cli_run.rs` are the entire surface. That is why I recorded NONE so often above — and it is the reason this class of defect reached four proven instances before being found."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1116,"cache_read_input_tokens":107476,"output_tokens":8748,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1116},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":8748,"cache_read_input_tokens":107476,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1116,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1116,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"requestId":"req_011CfFHM1vQrPoesgWYBjAD7","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"2da66f40-58ce-4bb6-818b-e25fdb4362e9","timestamp":"2026-09-20T19:59:40.073Z","effort":"medium","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/cklaus/projects/axon","sessionId":"b08bcf98-5d80-4faf-ab6a-34e0772b5071","version":"2.1.223","gitBranch":"main","slug":"valiant-dancing-bird"}
